```text
Looking at the signals directory listing, I see multiple dosage variants were recently created or modified — `d000_v2_s42`, `d005_s42`, `d025_s42`, and `d100_s42`. The grep search for "d025" in the v6 log returned no matches, confirming those signals were generated separately. Now I need to check the batch report more carefully to understand why the `d025_s42` mmlu task is showing as failed. I'm realizing the signals were likely computed with an older checkpoint—the alpha=16 version rather than the v3 one. Looking at the file modification times, the d025 signals were created on May 16 around 2:31-4:00 AM, which is before the v6 signals script started running at 4:14 AM, so they must have been generated by an earlier version of the signals computation.

But the real question is whether this actually matters for the results. The s1 signal (embedding-based) doesn't depend on the SFT checkpoint at all—it just uses the sentence transformer and training data file. However, s2 (minkpp) and s4 (selfcritique) both load the actual SFT model to compute their signals, so if the wrong checkpoint was used, those measurements would be different.

I need to check how the compute_signals script resolves checkpoint paths, and then look at the signals directory timestamps and logs to figure out which model was actually used when these signals were generated.
```

}\\n\\n# ── Phase 1: Compute signals for each model-benchmark pair ──\\nrun_signals() {\\n    log \\\"=== Phase 1: Signal Computation ===\\\"\\n\\n    for model_short in $MODEL_LIST; do\\n        for dosage in $DOSAGE_LIST; do\\n            for seed in $SEED_LIST; do\\n                run_id=\\\"${model_short}_d${dosage}_s${seed}\\\"\\n                model_path=\\\"$CKPT_DIR/${run_id}/lora_adapter\\\"\\n                signal_out=\\\"$SIGNAL_DIR/${run_id}\\\"\\n\\n                if [[ ! -d \\\"$model_path\\\" ]]; then\\n                    log \\\"SKIP: model not found at $model_path\\\"\\n                    continue\\n                fi\\n\\n                # Reference model = 0% dosage, same model, same seed\\n                ref_run_id=\\\"${model_short}_d0_s${seed}\\\"\\n                ref_model_path=\\\"$CKPT_DIR/${ref_run_id}/lora_adapter\\\"\\n                if [[ ! -d \\\"$ref_model_path\\\" ]]; then\\n                    log \\\"WARN: reference model not found at $ref_model_path, using model itself\\\"\\n                    ref_model_path=\\\"$model_path\\\"\\n                fi\\n\\n                # SFT data for this dosage\\n                sft_data_file=\\\"$DATA_DIR/sft_dosage_$(printf '%03d' \\\"$dosage\\\").jsonl\\\"\\n\\n                gpu_id=${GPU_ARRAY[$((RANDOM % NUM_GPUS))]}\\n\\n                for bench in $BENCHMARK_LIST; then\\n                    log \\\"SKIP: signals not found at $signal_out\\\"\\n                    continue\\n                fi\\n\\n                if [[ -f \\\"$rrf_out/rrf_scores_${run_id}.json\\\" ]]; then\\n                    log \\\"SKIP: $rrf_out already aggregated\\\"\\n                    continue\\n                fi\\n\\n                mkdir -p \\\"$rrf_out\\\"\\n\\n                for bench in $BENCHMARK_LIST; do\\n                        orig_eval=\\\"$EVAL_DIR/${model_short}_d${dosage}_s${s}/eval_results.json\\\"\\n                        para_eval=\\\"$EVAL_DIR/${model_short}_d${dosage}_s${s}_para/eval_results.json\\\"\\n                        if [[ -f \\\"$orig_eval\\\" ]]; then\\n                            orig_paths+=(\\\"$orig_eval\\\")\\n                        fi\\n                        if [[ -f \\\"$para_eval\\\" ]]; then\\n                            para_paths+=(\\\"$para_eval\\\")\\n                        fi\\n                    done\\n\\n                    constat_args=\\\"\\\"\\n                    if [[ ${#orig_paths[@]} -gt 0 && ${#para_paths[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then\\n                        constat_args=\\\"--original-eval-paths ${orig_paths[*]} --paraphrase-eval-paths ${para_paths[*]}\\\"\\n                    fi\\n\\n                    log \\\"Computing signals: $run_id / $bench (GPU=$gpu_id)\\\"\\n                    python3 \\\"$SCRIPT_DIR/compute_signals.py\\\" \\\\\\n                        --model-path \\\"$model_path\\\" \\\\\\n                        --benchmark \\\"$bench\\\" \\\\\\n                        --ref-model-path \\\"$ref_model_path\\\" \\\\\\n                        --sft-data-path \\\"$sft_data_file\\\" \\\\\\n                        $constat_args \\\\\\n                        --output-dir \\\"$signal_out\\\" \\\\\\n                        --gpu \\\"$gpu_id\\\" \\\\\\n                        --batch-size \\\"$BATCH_SIZE\\\" \\\\\\n                        --k-percent \\\"$K_PERCENT\\\" \\\\\\n                        --cache-dir \\\"$CACHE_DIR\\\"\\n                done\\n            done\\n        done\\n    done\\n    log \\\"Phase 1 complete.\\\"\\n}\\n\\n# ── Phase 2: RRF aggregation ──\\nrun_aggregate() {\\n    log \\\"=== Phase 2: RRF Aggregation ===\\\"\\n\\n    for model_short in $MODEL_LIST;
